Result: 在钙调蛋白（1CLL）和大肠杆菌天冬氨酸转氨甲酰酶（9EEH）上的评估表明，没有任何单一方法能在所有预算和oracle条件下一致地占优。O3在低oracle预算下表现最佳，而FK-steering和DPO在预算增加时性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个关于oracle预算感知引导（oracle budget-aware guidance）的实用参考，并将研究结果提炼为面向真实世界oracle预算约束下从业者的可操作建议。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [230] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多代理（agentic）的工作流，用于在工业生产规模上自动化现代Fortran代码的现代化改造。通过三个提示特化的代理角色在版本控制规范下协作，结合来自于领域本身的精确验证oracle，将安全委派的边界定义在oracle可见性的边界上。在GAMESS量子化学包的案例研究中，该工作流成功将56,448行Fortran 77代码转换为Fortran 2008，612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留Fortran代码现代化是体量问题：单个变换虽常规，但代码库庞大，且在整个计算科学领域中这项工作往往因为规模过大而无法完成。需要一种能够在生产规模上承担这项工作的方法，并衡量AI代理委派能达到的边界。

Method: 提出一个代理工作流，包含三个提示特化的Claude Code代理角色，在代理自己编写并修订的版本控制规范的指导下工作，人类只保留少量门控（gates）。安全性由来自于领域的精确验证oracle保证——即GAMESS标准测试套件的逐位（bit-for-bit）能量再现作为合并标准，第十二位小数的偏差即被视为失败。代理在隔离的worktree中运行，跨四代Claude模型工作。

Result: 案例研究将GAMESS的12个源文件、56,448行代码、225个子程序的固定格式Fortran 77转换为自由格式Fortran 2008。所有12个源文件通过51项验证测试（49项标准GAMESS测试加2项额外计算），612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零，且所有文件通过Jenkins持续集成测试。

Conclusion: 安全代理委派的边界恰好位于领域验证oracle能看到的范围之内。当存在精确、可自动化的验证标准时，多代理工作流能够以生产规模完成遗留代码的现代化改造，将大量常规变换委托给AI代理而不牺牲正确性。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [231] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VAKRA是一个企业级智能体基准测试，包含62个领域、8000多个可执行API，设置三种难度递增的任务场景；通过重新执行预测的工具调用验证正确性。评估发现即使最佳模型在单跳任务上仅达70.4%，在组合式API上降至50-51%，推理深度增加时性能下降超50%，策略约束问题暴露出严重缺陷（不可回答查询正确率仅2.4%）。失败主要集中于语言介导的推理环节（实体消歧、跨源对齐），而非工具调用机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中部署的智能体必须在结构化API和文档集合之间进行推理，但现有基准将这些能力孤立评估，缺乏同时覆盖API操作与知识检索的综合评测，因此需要构建一个更贴合企业真实场景的基准。

Method: 构建VAKRA基准：包含8000多个可执行API、覆盖62个领域，设计三种难度递增的任务设置（多样API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、带自然语言工具使用策略约束的多源推理）。采用固定ReAct框架隔离模型能力与智能体架构差异，通过重新执行预测的工具调用来验证正确性，并兼容多条有效路径。对前沿模型和开放权重模型进行评估，并进行轨迹分析定位失败环节。

Result: 最佳模型在单跳端点式任务上仅达70.4%，组合式API任务降至50-51%。推理深度增加时性能下降超50%。策略约束问题中不可回答查询正确率仅2.4%。轨迹分析显示失败集中在语言介导的推理过程（实体消歧、跨源grounding），而非工具调用机制本身。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 现有前沿及开放权重模型在企业级API+知识推理场景下表现严重不足，尤其是多跳组合推理和策略约束推理场景。语言介导的推理能力是当前主要瓶颈，提示后续研究应聚焦于实体消歧和跨源信息整合等语言推理能力的改进，而非工具调用机制。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [232] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化框架，利用检索增强生成（RAG）和大语言模型（LLM），从系统描述文档中自动构建动态主逻辑（DML）模型，并将其表示为知识图谱（KG-DML）。该框架将该方法从先前的小规模系统扩展到更大、更复杂的系统（如沸水堆低压冷却剂注入系统），并提出了多层级验证方法评估模型精度、召回率、逻辑门一致性和结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML框架能够将功能目标与底层结构元素关联，提供系统行为的层次化表示，但其构建依赖专家对技术文档的人工解读，限制了复杂系统的可扩展性。需要一种自动化方法来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用RAG和LLM作为使能工具，沿DML层级进行定向检索并自动构建KG-DML，同时保留功能依赖关系和显式逻辑关系。评估采用多层级验证方法，分别验证各层精度、召回率、逻辑门一致性和整体结构完整性。

Result: 将该框架应用于退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统，在多次重复运行中实现了一致的结构重建。结果表明自动化KG-DML构建能够将技术文档转化为可执行的功能模型，支持诊断推理、安全评估、向上故障传播和向下依赖追踪。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建技术可行且具有一致性，可将技术文档高效转化为可执行的功能模型，为复杂系统的诊断和可靠性分析提供支持，有效解决了DML构建的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [A Geodesic Cut-Cell Prior for Neural Skinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11272)
*Wenchao Ma,Surya Dwarakanath,Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Dario Kneubühler,Haomiao Jiang,Sharon X. Huang,Hsueh-Ti Derek Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出"cut-cell skinning"（切割单元蒙皮），一种几何先验，用于增强数据驱动的蒙皮权重生成。通过基于图结构的快速体积测地距离近似，实现比优化求解器快数个数量级的速度，且对拓扑伪影具有鲁棒性。将该先验集成到现有神经蒙皮模型中，在多个方法上取得一致改进并达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的蒙皮权重生成方法虽然能产生高质量结果，但缺乏经典几何方法（如基于体积测地距离的蒙皮权重计算）的泛化能力。现有几何方法（如基于cage或voxel的方案）易受拓扑伪影影响且计算开销大，难以用于大规模机器学习流程。因此需要一种既稳健高效、又能补充数据驱动方法泛化性不足的几何先验。

Method: 提出cut-cell skinning几何先验，核心思想是通过快速图结构近似体积测地距离。该方法利用cut-cell结构构建图，实现了对经典蒙皮权重计算中重要体积测地距离的高效逼近，相比基于优化的求解器获得数量级的加速，同时对cage或voxel方法中常见的拓扑伪影具有鲁棒性。

Result: 该方法相比优化求解器实现数量级加速，对拓扑伪影具有韧性。将该先验集成到多种现有神经蒙皮模型中，在各类方法上均获得一致的性能提升，取得了最先进的结果（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: cut-cell skinning作为一种高效、稳健的几何先验，能有效桥接数据驱动与经典几何蒙皮方法之间的泛化差距，可作为通用组件增强现有神经蒙皮模型，未来有望被广泛采用于大规模蒙皮权重学习流程中。

Abstract: We introduce cut-cell skinning, a geometric prior designed to augment data-driven skinning weight generation. While data-driven methods show promise in producing high-quality skinning weights, they often lack the generalizability of classic geometric approaches. To bridge this gap, we propose a geometric prior that can be robustly computed for in-the-wild meshes and is efficient for large-scale machine learning workflows. The key idea of our cut-cell skinning is a fast graph-based approximation of the volumetric geodesics distances, motivated by their importance in classic skinning weight computation. Our method achieves orders of magnitude speedup compared to optimization-based solvers and remains resilient to topological artifacts common in cage- or voxel-based alternatives. We demonstrate the efficacy of the cut-cell skinning prior by integrating it into recent neural skinning models, showing consistent improvements across existing methods and achieving state-of-the-art results. Project page: https://wenchao-m.github.io/CutCell.github.io/

</details>


### [234] [MVFM-3DAD: Multi-view Flow Matching for 3D Anomaly Detection via Density Proxy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12148)
*Liangwei Li,Lin Liu,Jing Zhang,Xiaohui Du,Ruqian Hao,Xinwei Li,Hanzhe Liang,Juanxiu Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出MVFM-3DAD，一种基于流的3D异常检测框架，将3DAD重构为对正常数据分布的密度代理估计。通过双向几何投影器（BGP）在点云与多视角表示间转换，利用流引导密度代理估计器（FDPE）估计参考密度，并在Real3D-AD和MVTec3D-AD上超越现有最强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D异常检测方法主要分为基于记忆库检索和基于重建两类，但前者受限于存储正常特征的覆盖范围，后者可能学习恒等捷径从而也能重建异常输入。这些局限促使研究者探索基于密度评估的方法，即判断测试样本是否遵循学习的正常数据分布。

Method: 提出MVFM-3DAD流式框架，将3DAD看作正常数据分布上的密度代理估计。包含两个核心组件：1）双向几何投影器（BGP）：前向过程将不规则点云转换为结构化多视角表示，后向过程将多视角密度估计映射回对应3D点；2）流引导密度代理估计器（FDPE）：为每个视角特征估计参考密度。异常特征通过其终态正常性（terminal normality）识别，无需输入重建或显式雅可比计算。

Result: 在Real3D-AD和MVTec3D-AD两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MVFM-3DAD优于最强的对比方法，且由于无需重建输入或评估显式雅可比矩阵，算法简单高效。

Conclusion: MVFM-3DAD通过密度代理估计范式成功克服了记忆库方法与重建方法的局限性，提供了一种更简单、高效且性能卓越的3D异常检测方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: In 3D anomaly detection (3DAD), most existing methods rely on Memory bank retrieval or reconstruction. However, memory-based methods are constrained by the coverage of stored normal features, while reconstruction-based methods may learn identity shortcuts that also reconstruct anomalous inputs well. These limitations motivate a density-oriented approach that evaluates whether a test sample follows the learned normal distribution. To this end, we propose MVFM-3DAD, a flow-based framework that reframes 3DAD as density proxy estimation over the normal data distribution. MVFM-3DAD introduces a Bidirectional Geometric Projector (BGP), whose forward process converts irregular point clouds into structured multi-view representations. The Flow-guided Density Proxy Estimator (FDPE) estimates a reference density for each view feature, after which the backward process of BGP maps these multi-view density estimates to their corresponding 3D points. Building on it, anomalous features can be identified by their terminal normality. Unlike conventional flow-based likelihood estimation, our formulation requires neither input reconstruction nor explicit Jacobian evaluation, yielding a simple and efficient anomaly-scoring mechanism. Extensive experiments show that MVFM-3DAD outperforms the strongest competing methods on Real3D-AD and MVTec3D-AD. Code is available at https://github.com/lil-wayne-0319/MV3D-AD

</details>
